Literal planning now covers six LOCALSTOCK functional cases and 55 optional
capture cases (11 Workspace,20 StoreLive,24 founder), not 61 screenshot cases.
The separate 23 always-skipped historical cases retain individual disposition
requirements. The four default report plans are unchanged. Optional plans are
layout-only, case-sensitive and use fixed exact-name selectors/defines; these
planning fixtures do not prove Flutter selected or executed those cases.

Default report, capture sidecar and first source marker now independently bind
execution identity. Legacy missing identity means only the exact default plan;
supplemental identity cannot pass schema1 default qualification. Native argument
and runtime-define arrays require original array shape, typed string elements,
equal counts and ordinal element equality, not delimiter-joined equivalence.
Selectors reject trailing LF/CRLF. Source-marker rejection reads a closed file
snapshot, avoiding the reproduced lazy-iterator handle leak on both hosts.
Actual producer remains default-only; no supplemental capture is admitted.
